{"file_path": "C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu\\PLAN-post-question-explanation.md", "content": "# Plan: post-question Explanation, LSAT Lab shape, AI Tutor off\n_Locked via grill-with-docs on 2026-09-12 by Claude + Joshua. Revised after Codex round 1. Terms per `src/components/drill/CONTEXT.md` (Answer, Explanation, Argument Breakdown, Choice Note, Reset Response, AI Tutor)._\n\n## Goal\n\nOn the official drill surface (`/drill`), the student reviews a question the\nway LSAT Lab shows it: the **Answer** pill and the **Explanation** pill are\nindependent on/off toggles, the **Argument Breakdown** sits under the\nstimulus (and is repeated in the drawer), every **Choice Note** is a dropdown\nunder its choice with a chevron, and a **Reset Response** control clears the\npick. A wrong submit colours the choices and does nothing else. The **AI\nTutor** is off for every student behind one switch, because the Question\nExplanation is authored LSAT Lab content already in Postgres\n(`lr_explanations`, `lr_explanation_steps`, `lr_explanation_choice_notes`)\nand the generated chat was failing on screen. Alongside the code change, a\ncensus of every bank question with no authored prose, keyed PrepTest /\nSection / Question.\n\n## Scope of modes (added after round 1)\n\nThe behaviours below apply to the **explicit-submit modes** on the official\nsurface: adaptive Smart Drill and the fixed queues (guided drills, custom\ntype drills, flagged / library queues), which are the modes that render\n`OfficialSmartDrillBar`. Predicate, written once as\n`reviewControlsAvailable = usesOfficialLayout && !revealSealed && !isFullSection && !isPracticeSetMode`.\n\nUntouched: sealed runs and practice-test sittings (selection, revision and\nnavigation exactly as today), full-section mode (its opt-in\n`sectionRevealEnabled` reveal stays as is), Bootcamp practice sets\n(`isPracticeSetMode`, where a selection *is* the attempt and\n`handleAnswerSelect` rewrites `session.attempts` by design), and the legacy\n`DrillTopBar` layout.\n\n## Approach\n\nWork on a fresh branch off `main` (`feat/post-question-explanation`); the\ncurrent worktree branch `feat/calendar-strip-deeplink` carries 87 modified\nfiles from another session and none of them are touched here.\n\n### 1. One switch for the AI Tutor\n\n- `src/components/drill/aiTutorFlag.ts` exports `AI_TUTOR_ENABLED = false`\n  with a comment naming the surfaces it gates and the date.\n- The constant gates **mounting and launching**, not just initial state:\n  - `renderTutorOverlay` returns `null` when the flag is off, regardless of\n    `tutorChatOpen`.\n  - `openTutorForCurrent`, and the three wrong-answer launch sites\n    (`Drill.tsx:1849`, `:1957`, `:2105`) plus the fixed-queue submit wrapper\n    (`:4261-4283`) all read `AI_TUTOR_ENABLED && tutorMode`. Concretely:\n    `resolveTutorLaunch` gains a required `flagEnabled` argument and returns\n    `shouldOpen: false` when it is off.\n  - The legacy layout's tutor controls (`:4416-4431`) are rendered only when\n    the flag is on.\n  - `tutorMode` initial state stays `true` (its meaning is \"the student's\n    preference\"); the flag is the master. `tutorToggleVisible` on the frame\n    stays `false` as it is today, so flipping the flag on restores exactly\n    the pre-change behaviour and nothing more.\n- `DrillExplanationTab` receives `onAskTutor` only when the flag is on. Its\n  no-prose copy becomes: \"No written explanation was published for this\n  question.\" (Not \"video only\": see step 7 on why we cannot claim that.)\n- `renderVoiceCoachChip` returns `null` when the flag is off.\n- `TutorChatModal` is not edited.\n\n### 2. Answer and Explanation become independent\n\nToday they are one switch with two reaches (`toggleExplanation` at\n`Drill.tsx:3625-3640` hides the Answer when Explanation closes, and opening\nExplanation goes through the reveal path; the drawer and Choice Notes also\ngate on `currentRevealed`).\n\n- New per-qid state `explanationOpenByQid: Map<string, boolean>` in\n  `Drill.tsx`. `explanationOpen` is derived from it, no longer from\n  `notesOpen && drawerTab === 'explanation'`.\n- `toggleAnswerReveal`: flips `showAnswerRevealed[qid]` only. Never touches\n  the drawer or `explanationOpenByQid`.\n- `toggleExplanation`: flips `explanationOpenByQid[qid]` only. Turning it on\n  also opens the drawer on the Explanation tab (Joshua wants both places);\n  turning it off closes the drawer if it is showing that tab. Never touches\n  `showAnswerRevealed`.\n- The unscored-reveal confirm (`handleRevealRequest`, pressing either pill\n  before any attempt) still fires for both pills, and marks the qid unscored\n  as today; after \"Show me\" it flips the pill that was pressed, not both.\n- `useLrExplanation` is fetched when `explanationOpen`, not when\n  `currentRevealed`. `DrillExplanationTab.revealed` and\n  `OfficialQuestionPane.inlineNotes` are driven by `explanationOpen`.\n- `OfficialQuestionPane`: the Choice Note dropdowns render when\n  `inlineNotes` is non-null, independent of `answerRevealed`. Green/red\n  colouring remains driven by `answerRevealed` alone. The four combinations\n  (off/off, Answer only, Explanation only, both) each have a test.\n\n### 3. A wrong submit colours and stops; nothing locks\n\n- In the explicit-submit modes, every submit (right or wrong) sets\n  `showAnswerRevealed[qid] = true` (Answer on) and nothing else. That is\n  Joshua's rule: wrong pick red, correct choice green, Explanation stays\n  closed.\n- Consequence for `autoReveal`: it becomes a no-op in these modes. The\n  switch is **removed from `OfficialSmartDrillBar`** (prop kept optional,\n  control not rendered when `reviewControlsAvailable`). Its stored\n  preference is left alone. Flagged for Joshua as a visible change.\n- `answerLocked` is no longer set to `true` by any reveal or submit path in\n  these modes (`:1851`, `:1958`, `:1989`, `:2113`, `:4271`). It stays a\n  legacy-layout concern.\n- `handleAnswerSelect` guard: delete `if (!tutorMode && showSolution) return;`\n  outright. The remaining guard is unchanged:\n  `if (!isPracticeSetMode && !isRetryAfterWrong && answerLocked) return;`,\n  and since nothing in these modes sets `answerLocked` any more, picks stay\n  live. Sealed runs and sittings keep whatever they do today, verbatim.\n- `OfficialQuestionPane`: choice buttons are no longer `disabled` when\n  `answerRevealed`; they are disabled only when `isEliminated`. The\n  cross-out control follows the same rule.\n\n### 4. Practice picks after an attempt\n\n- New state `practicePickByQid: Map<string, string>`. In the explicit-submit\n  modes, once `session.attempts.has(qid)`, `handleAnswerSelect` writes to\n  `practicePickByQid` instead of `selectedAnswer`, and never touches\n  `session.attempts`.\n- `OfficialQuestionPane.selectedAnswer` receives\n  `practicePickByQid.get(qid) ?? selectedAnswer`.\n- `OfficialSmartDrillBar.primaryAction`: `'submit'` when there is an\n  unattempted pick **or** a practice pick that has not been checked;\n  `'next'` otherwise. A practice Submit sets `showAnswerRevealed[qid] = true`\n  and marks the practice pick checked. It never calls `persistAttempt`,\n  never writes `session.attempts`, never touches journal, adaptive stats,\n  or resume rows. Student work is never modified after the fact.\n- The adaptive existing-attempt branch (`:1985-1995`) stops restoring\n  `selectedAnswer` from the recorded attempt when a practice pick exists.\n\n### 5. Reset Response\n\n- `OfficialQuestionPane` gets `onResetResponse?: () => void` and renders a\n  right-aligned ghost button \"Reset Response\" under the `<ul>`, `disabled`\n  when the effective selection is empty. Rendered only when the prop is\n  present (i.e. `reviewControlsAvailable`).\n- Handler: clears `practicePickByQid[qid]` if attempted, else clears\n  `selectedAnswer`. Touches nothing else: not the timer, `session.attempts`,\n  `showAnswerRevealed`, `explanationOpenByQid`, drawer, journal, or resume.\n\n### 6. Persistence is honest and single-flight\n\n- `handleSubmitNonAdaptive` (`:1744-1785`) currently toasts on a failed\n  save and still writes `session.attempts` and adaptive stats. Change: on\n  `!saved`, toast and `return null` **before** any state write, so the\n  question remains submittable and the guard in step 4 never mistakes a\n  failed save for a committed attempt.\n- Add `nonAdaptiveCommitInFlightRef: Set<qid>` mirroring the adaptive\n  guard at `:2012`; the fixed-queue `onSubmit` returns early if the qid is\n  in flight, and `primaryDisabled` is true while it is.\n- Test: double-click Submit with a delayed `persistAttempt` mock writes once.\n\n### 7. Argument Breakdown under the stimulus\n\n- New `src/components/drill/official/ArgumentBreakdown.tsx`, props\n  `{ data, isLoading, isError, activeStyle, onStyleChange, fontScale }`.\n  A `border-t` rule, the style pills only when `styles.length > 1`, then\n  labelled steps (label on its own line, slate-700 600-weight ~0.85 × base;\n  body sans at base size, 1.6 line-height).\n- Shared helper `resolveBreakdownSteps(data, activeStyle)` in\n  `useLrExplanation.ts` carries the borrow-Standard rule; both\n  `DrillExplanationTab` and `ArgumentBreakdown` call it.\n- `OfficialQuestionPane` gets `belowStimulus?: React.ReactNode`. The slot is\n  a **sibling** of the stimulus `div`, inside a new left-column wrapper\n  `flex flex-col overflow-y-auto`; the stimulus `div` loses only its own\n  `overflow-y-auto` (the wrapper scrolls) and keeps every other class:\n  serif, `whitespace-pre-wrap`, `[&>*]:max-w-[52ch]`, padding, highlight\n  handlers. The slot has its own `mt-6 px-6 sm:pl-9 sm:pr-8 font-sans\n  whitespace-normal max-w-[60ch]`.\n- `Drill.tsx` passes `belowStimulus={explanationOpen ? <ArgumentBreakdown …/> : null}`.\n- The drawer's Explanation tab stays, rendered from the same data.\n\n### 8. Choice Notes as dropdowns\n\n- `InlineChoiceExplanation` gains `open: boolean` and `onToggle: () => void`.\n  Header row (tag chip, \"% of students\", \"Your answer\") always renders when\n  any of the three exists. The chevron (`ChevronUp`/`ChevronDown`,\n  `aria-expanded`, \"Hide explanation for A\" / \"Show explanation for A\") and\n  the prose render **only when `note.trim().length > 0`**. So a selected\n  choice with no published note gets its \"Your answer\" chip and no\n  dropdown; `isInlineChoiceEmpty` keeps its job (nothing at all when there\n  is no tag, no percent, no trimmed note, and not selected).\n- The choice grid gains a 4th column so the chevron aligns under the\n  Check/X icon column.\n- `OfficialQuestionPane` owns `openLetters: Set<string>`, reset to every\n  letter with a trimmed note whenever `inlineNotes` becomes non-null or the\n  qid changes (pressing Explanation always opens expanded, per the\n  recording). Turning Explanation off discards it.\n- The \"ALWAYS VISIBLE\" comment at the top of `InlineChoiceExplanation` is\n  rewritten to state the new rule and why.\n\n### 9. Tooltips on the pills\n\n`OfficialSessionStrip`: Answer `title` = \"Show correct answer\" / \"Hide\ncorrect answer\"; Explanation `title` = \"Show explanations\" / \"Hide\nexplanations\". Copy from the LSAT Lab recording.\n\n### 10. Census of questions with no authored explanation\n\n- `scripts/missing-explanations-census.mjs`. Loads the service key the way\n  `scripts/db-pull.mjs` does, and **pages every table at 1000 rows with\n  `Range` headers until a short page**, the same loop `db-pull.mjs` uses.\n  Aborts (non-zero exit, nothing written) on any query error or if a table's\n  row count disagrees with a `count=exact` head request.\n- Reads the bank from `public/data/PT*-LR.json` (156 files).\n- Shared predicate: a question **has authored prose** iff at least one\n  `lr_explanation_steps.body` or `lr_explanation_choice_notes.body` for it is\n  non-empty after `trim()`. The same trimmed rule replaces the current\n  `hasAuthored` in `useLrExplanation.ts` (which counts rows, not prose).\n- Reasons are exactly what the data proves: `not ingested` (no\n  `lr_questions` row for the address) or `no authored prose` (row exists,\n  nothing passes the predicate). No \"video only\" label: nothing in the\n  schema records a video, and the 14 known video-only questions (PT143,\n  PT146 per the 2026-08-03 migration) are simply a subset of \"no authored\n  prose\". The report notes that fact in its header.\n- Output `ai-os/wiki/explanations/MISSING-EXPLANATIONS.md`: summary counts\n  (bank total, ingested, with prose, missing by reason), then one table per\n  PrepTest, rows `PT · S · Q · question type · reason`, sorted PT, section,\n  question. Run once, committed to the vault, not the app repo.\n\n### 11. Tests\n\n- `OfficialQuestionPane.test.tsx`: the four Answer × Explanation\n  combinations; Explanation on → every noted letter expanded; chevron folds\n  one; new qid re-expands; a selected choice with no note shows the chip and\n  no chevron; choices stay clickable while `answerRevealed`; Reset Response\n  disabled with no pick, clears the pick, leaves `answerRevealed` alone.\n- `InlineChoiceExplanation`: closed renders header, no prose; whitespace\n  note renders no chevron.\n- `tutorBehavior.test.ts` (new): `resolveTutorLaunch` with\n  `flagEnabled=false` never opens, whatever `tutorModeEnabled` is.\n- Drill-level: wrong submit in a fixed queue → Answer on, Explanation off,\n  `answerLocked` false, no tutor; failed save leaves the question\n  submittable and writes nothing; practice Submit after Reset writes no\n  attempt; double Submit persists once.\n- Existing tests asserting the tutor opens after a wrong answer are updated.\n\n### 12. Verify in the browser\n\nPreview server, at 1280, 1024 and 390 widths and with the drawer docked:\n- Smart Drill and a guided drill: submit wrong → red + green only;\n  Explanation → breakdown under stimulus, dropdowns open; fold one;\n  Explanation off → dropdowns and breakdown gone, colours stay; Answer off →\n  colours gone, dropdowns unaffected; Reset Response → pick cleared, timer\n  running; pick again + Submit → colours, no second attempt row (check\n  `drill_attempts` or the session map in devtools).\n- Cold load with Explanation already on for a qid (refresh mid-review): no\n  flash of the always-open layout.\n- A sealed run and a practice set: selection and navigation unchanged.\n- Computed style of the stimulus `div` (font-family, font-size, padding,\n  max-width) identical before and after, read with `getComputedStyle`.\n- Screenshots for Joshua.\n\n## Key decisions & tradeoffs\n\n- **Flag gates mounting, not just state.** Round 1 showed the state-only\n  version leaked through the legacy controls.\n- **Answer and Explanation decoupled at the state level.** The old \"one\n  switch, two reaches\" design is the thing Joshua's recording contradicts.\n- **Every submit turns Answer on; auto-reveal control removed.** A choice\n  between Joshua's rule and the existing switch; his rule wins, the switch\n  would lie. Visible change, called out for him.\n- **Practice picks live in their own map.** The only way \"nothing locks\"\n  and \"student work is never modified\" are both true.\n- **Failed save = no state write.** Today's behaviour records an attempt the\n  DB never saw; the new guard would have frozen it. Fixing the root is\n  cheaper than special-casing.\n- **Dropdowns open by default, chevron only with prose.** Keeps the old\n  reason (no click tax to read five notes) and honours the empty-render rule.\n- **Census says only what the data proves.** \"Video only\" was an inference;\n  the schema has no video column.\n- **Flag, not deletion** of the tutor. Joshua wants it back later.\n\n## Risks / open questions\n\n- Removing the auto-reveal switch: Joshua has not seen this consequence;\n  confirm before build.\n- `showSolution` / `showReviewButton` remain referenced by the legacy layout;\n  the audit may find that the official surface still reads them somewhere.\n- Breakdown label sizing vs LSAT Lab is a taste call; verify on screen.\n- The census needs the service key from `~/ai-os/.env`; if absent it says so\n  and exits non-zero.\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- `TutorChatModal`, the edge function, token budgeting.\n- Sealed runs, practice-test sittings, full-section mode, Bootcamp practice\n  sets, the legacy `DrillTopBar` layout: behaviour preserved, not redesigned.\n- Reading Comprehension (`RcChoiceDisclosure`) and the Classroom\n  `LRQuestionModal`.\n- Video explanations: no toggle, no hosting, no claim.\n- Authoring the missing explanations.\n"}

## user

Tool result:
The revision addresses the tutor master switch, empty-note handling, prose detection, census pagination, and broader browser coverage. These issues still require changes:

1. **High — Explanation-only mode mislabels the correct choice.** `OfficialQuestionPane` computes `isCorrect = answerRevealed && correctAnswer === key` and passes it to `InlineChoiceExplanation`, which uses it for “Why this is correct/wrong.” With Answer off, the correct choice receives “Why this is wrong.”  
   **Fix:** Pass factual correctness separately from whether Answer colouring is enabled.

2. **High — Reset can restore the original pick instead of clearing it.** Step 5 says to clear `practicePickByQid[qid]`, but deleting that entry makes `practicePickByQid.get(qid) ?? selectedAnswer` fall back to the recorded selection.  
   **Fix:** Explicitly store `''` as the attempted question’s cleared practice response; distinguish an absent override from an empty selection.

3. **High — Save failure still permits reveal, navigation, and completion.** Returning null from `handleSubmitNonAdaptive` does not stop its callers: the submit wrapper continues revealing, `commitPendingSelection` discards the result, and `officialNext` proceeds to navigation or section completion (`Drill.tsx:3844–3884`, `:4264`).  
   **Fix:** Propagate a success/failure result through every caller and stop reveal, navigation, and completion on failure.

4. **High — Single-flight protection remains incomplete.** Step 6 guards the fixed-queue Submit wrapper, but Previous/Next/navigation also invoke persistence. Submit followed by navigation can still duplicate a write; a ref mutation alone also does not rerender `primaryDisabled`.  
   **Fix:** Put the guard inside the shared commit function, expose reactive pending state, and make navigation await the same pending commit.

5. **High — Practice selection has not replaced all selection consumers.** `handleEliminateAnswer` still checks and clears `selectedAnswer` (`Drill.tsx:1390`), while the displayed practice answer lives elsewhere. Crossing out a practice pick can leave it selected; Submit disabling also currently reads the original selection.  
   **Fix:** Define one effective-selection accessor and route selection toggling, elimination, validation, Reset, and practice Submit through it, with explicit checked-state transitions.

6. **High — The proposed primary-action rule drops the unscored exemption.** Step 2 preserves unscored peeking, but step 4 offers Submit for an “unattempted pick” without excluding `currentUnscored`. A student can view the explanation, select the answer, and reach a scoring path unless another guard is added.  
   **Fix:** Give `currentUnscored` precedence in both action selection and commit handlers; test peeking, hiding both pills, selecting, and navigating.

7. **Medium — “Untouched modes” conflicts with unconditional shared changes.** Step 3 deletes the legacy `!tutorMode && showSolution` guard outright and globally unlocks `OfficialQuestionPane`; step 2 globally replaces the explanation-fetch gate. These instructions exceed `reviewControlsAvailable`.  
   **Fix:** Scope changed behaviour explicitly through the predicate and optional pane props whose defaults preserve existing consumers.

8. **Medium — The cross-out rule can make elimination irreversible.** “The cross-out control follows the same rule” as choice buttons means disabling it when `isEliminated`, which disables Restore. Also, revealed verdict icons currently replace that control entirely.  
   **Fix:** Keep Restore enabled and specify how elimination controls coexist with correctness icons after Answer is on.

9. **Medium — The drawer now has an unsynchronized Explanation entry point.** Its tab handler still calls `setDrawerTab` directly (`Drill.tsx:4088`). Selecting Explanation through Menu will not set the new map, fetch content, or pass the new visibility gate—even after an attempt.  
   **Fix:** Route drawer Explanation-tab activation through the same question-specific open/confirmation handler as the pill.

10. **Medium — Two verification claims are invalid.** The browser check names `drill_attempts`, but persistence writes `attempts`; checking only the session map cannot detect duplicate database rows. Refresh also cannot retain a newly introduced in-memory Explanation map without persistence.  
    **Fix:** Verify actual `attempts` writes and replace the refresh scenario with a cold-cache first-open test unless restoring review visibility is explicitly implemented.

VERDICT: REVISE

Most prior findings are now addressed, but four concrete issues remain:

1. **High — The scope predicate cannot be used as written.** The code defines `isOfficialLayout` at `Drill.tsx:3751`, inside the late rendering branch; the plan names nonexistent `usesOfficialLayout` and needs `reviewControlsAvailable` inside handlers declared around line 1329. A predicate declared in the rendering branch is out of scope there.  
   **Fix:** Derive a null-safe `reviewControlsAvailable` before the shared handlers using the actual `isOfficialLayout` condition and stable mode inputs.

2. **High — Drawer tab routing uses toggle semantics where ensure-open semantics are required.** If Explanation is already on while the drawer shows Notes or Details, clicking the drawer’s Explanation tab would call `toggleExplanation` and turn Explanation off instead of selecting its content.  
   **Fix:** Split `openExplanation` from `toggleExplanation`; the drawer tab ensures Explanation is on and selects the tab, while only the pill toggles it off.

3. **Medium — The single-flight design does not identify the promise that navigation awaits.** A `Set<qid>` plus `pendingCommitQid` can detect an existing commit but cannot return its result. If Next arrives during Submit, it needs the exact pending promise to learn whether persistence succeeded before navigating.  
   **Fix:** Store `Map<qid, Promise<CommitResult>>`, return the existing promise from the shared commit function, and clear it in `finally`.

4. **Medium — The database verification can report a false duplicate.** The `attempts` table records historical attempts, so the same user and qid may legitimately have rows from earlier runs. “No second row for that user + qid” does not isolate this interaction.  
   **Fix:** Compare the row-count delta around the practice Submit or filter by the newly captured attempt ID/session timestamp and assert that Reset plus practice Submit adds zero rows.

VERDICT: REVISE

1. **High — Restore still has no concrete interactive control for an eliminated verdict choice.** The plan says the Check/X icon occupies the existing cross-out position, then says Restore remains available “from the choice’s hover area.” The current badge and text buttons are disabled for eliminated choices, and the verdict icon is a noninteractive `<span>`, so that description does not identify anything the student can press.  
   **Fix:** Render a separate, labelled Restore button for an eliminated correct choice or wrong pick, with its own grid position and keyboard focus.

   **Fix:** When confirmation is required, change no explanation or drawer state until “Show me”; Cancel must leave both unchanged, and add a test for that path.

Here is the point again, in short sentences.

**Where we are**

The plan is written. Codex reviewed it five times. One question is left. Only you can answer it.

**The words**

- **Submit** is the moment the student presses the Submit button after she picks a choice. The app records that choice as her attempt.
- **Answer** is the pill at the top of the drill screen. When Answer is on, the correct choice shows a green badge. If the student picked a different choice, her choice shows a red badge. When Answer is off, no badge shows colour. That is what I meant by "coloured choices": the green and red badges.

**What you told me**

You said: when a student submits a wrong choice, her choice goes red and the correct choice goes green. The Explanation stays closed.

I wrote that as: **a Submit turns Answer on.** Right or wrong, the badges show colour after Submit.

**The conflict**

The Smart Drill bar has a switch today. It is called **auto-reveal**. When a student turns it off, she can Submit and see no colour. The correct choice stays hidden. She can move on without knowing if she was right.

Your rule and that switch cannot both be true. If a Submit always turns Answer on, the switch does nothing.

**The question**

Pick one:

1. **Remove the auto-reveal switch.** Every Submit shows the green and red badges. This is what the plan says now. My recommendation.
2. **Keep the switch.** A Submit shows the badges only when auto-reveal is on. When it is off, the student sees no colour until she presses the Answer pill herself.

Which one?

The **Answer** pill and the **Explanation** pill in\nthe session strip are independent on/off toggles. The **Argument Breakdown**\nsits under the stimulus (and is repeated in the drawer). Every **Choice\nNote** is a dropdown under its choice with a chevron. A **Reset Response**\ncontrol clears the pick. A fixed drill (guided drill, small drill, custom\nqueue) has no Submit: its bottom bar is the **Answer Navigator** (left\narrow, ovals, right arrow), the same bar a full section uses, and nothing\nshows colour until the student presses Answer. Alongside the code change, a census of every\nbank question with no authored prose, keyed PrepTest / Section / Question.\n\n## Scope of modes\n\nThree groups of modes on the official surface. Joshua reports Smart Drill's question selection is broken;\n  that is a separate job and this plan does not touch it.\n- **Untouched**: sealed runs and practice-test sittings, full-section mode\n  (already on the Answer Navigator; its opt-in `sectionRevealEnabled`\n  reveal stays), Bootcamp practice sets (`isPracticeSetMode`, where a\n  selection *is* the attempt by design), and the classic `DrillTopBar`\n  layout. **Every parent-level change below is branched on\n  `reviewControlsAvailable`, with today's code as the else branch**, and\n  full-section + practice-set runs get regression tests on both pills.\n\nThe existing `isOfficialLayout` inside the render branch at `:3751` is\ndeleted in favour of the hoisted one (same expression, same inputs).\n\n## Approach\n\nWork on a fresh branch off `main` (`feat/post-question-explanation`); the\ncurrent worktree branch `feat/calendar-strip-deeplink` carries 87 modified\nfiles from another session and none of them are touched here.\n\n### 1. One switch for the AI Tutor\n\n- `src/components/drill/aiTutorFlag.ts` exports `AI_TUTOR_ENABLED = false`\n  with a comment naming the surfaces it gates and the date.\n- The constant gates **mounting and launching**, not just initial state:\n  - `renderTutorOverlay` returns `null` when the flag is off, regardless of\n    `tutorChatOpen`.\n  - `openTutorForCurrent`, the three wrong-answer launch sites\n    (`Drill.tsx:1849`, `:1957`, `:2105`) and the fixed-queue submit wrapper\n    (`:4261-4283`, which step 3 deletes anyway) all read\n    `AI_TUTOR_ENABLED && tutorMode`. `resolveTutorLaunch` gains a required\n    `flagEnabled` argument and returns `shouldOpen: false` when it is off.\n  - The legacy layout's tutor controls (`:4416-4431`) render only when the\n    flag is on.\n  - `tutorMode` initial state stays `true`; the flag is the master.\n    `tutorToggleVisible` on the frame stays `false` as today, so flipping\n    the flag on restores exactly the pre-change behaviour.\n- `DrillExplanationTab` receives `onAskTutor` only when the flag is on. In the\n  `reviewControlsAvailable` modes,\n  `explanationOpen = !!explanationOpenByQid.get(qid)` and the explanation\n  fetch is enabled by `explanationOpen`; elsewhere both stay\n  `notesOpen && drawerTab === 'explanation'` and `currentRevealed`, as now.\n  The strip receives `onShowAnswer` / `onExplanation` bound to the new\n  handlers only when `reviewControlsAvailable`, else the existing ones.\n- `toggleAnswerReveal(qid)`: flips `showAnswerRevealed[qid]` only. The alternative (asking \"unscored?\" about a\n  visibly answered question) is worse in every case.\n- **Flag gates mounting, not just state.** Round 1 showed the state-only\n  version leaked through the legacy controls.\n- **Answer and Explanation decoupled at the state level**, only in the\n  `reviewControlsAvailable` modes, with today's code as the else branch.\n- **Practice state is a map with an explicit empty pick.** A cleared\n  practice response and \"never touched\" must be different values or Reset\n  restores the recorded answer.\n- **Failures stop the caller chain.** A null from the commit function is\n  only honest if nothing downstream ignores it.\n- **Pane changes are opt-in props.** `choicesStayLive`, `belowStimulus`,\n  `onResetResponse` all default to today's behaviour.\n- **Dropdowns open by default, chevron only with prose.**\n- **Census says only what the data proves.**\n- **Flag, not deletion** of the tutor. Joshua wants it back later.\n\n## Risks / open questions\n\n- `officialPrev` / `handleJumpTo` may not commit a pending pick today; the\n  audit in step 3 settles it.\n- `showSolution` / `showReviewButton` remain referenced by the legacy\n  layout; the audit may find the official surface still reads them.\n- Breakdown label sizing vs LSAT Lab is a taste call; verify on screen.\n- The census needs the service key from `~/ai-os/.env`; if absent it says\n  so and exits non-zero.\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- Smart Drill's question selection bug and its bottom bar.\n- `TutorChatModal`, the edge function, token budgeting.\n- Sealed runs, practice-test sittings, full-section mode, Bootcamp practice\n  sets, the classic `DrillTopBar` layout: behaviour preserved.\n- Reading Comprehension (`RcChoiceDisclosure`) and the Classroom\n  `LRQuestionModal`.\n- Video explanations: no toggle, no hosting, no claim.\n- Authoring the missing explanations.\n"}

Every **Choice\nNote** is a dropdown under its choice with a chevron. A **Reset Response**\ncontrol clears the pick. Gets the shared improvements only: the pills decoupled, the\n  breakdown under the stimulus, Choice Note dropdowns, Reset Response, and\n  no AI Tutor. Joshua reports Smart Drill's question selection is broken;\n  that is a separate job and this plan does not touch it.\n- **Untouched**: sealed runs and practice-test sittings, full-section mode\n  (already on the Answer Navigator; its opt-in `sectionRevealEnabled`\n  reveal stays), Bootcamp practice sets (`isPracticeSetMode`, where a\n  selection *is* the attempt by design), and the classic `DrillTopBar`\n  layout. **Every parent-level change below is branched on\n  `reviewControlsAvailable`, with today's code as the else branch**, and\n  full-section + practice-set runs get regression tests on both pills.\n\nThe existing `isOfficialLayout` inside the render branch at `:3751` is\ndeleted in favour of the hoisted one (same expression, same inputs).\n\n## Approach\n\nWork on a fresh branch off `main` (`feat/post-question-explanation`); the\ncurrent worktree branch `feat/calendar-strip-deeplink` carries 87 modified\nfiles from another session and none of them are touched here.\n\n### 1. One switch for the AI Tutor\n\n- `src/components/drill/aiTutorFlag.ts` exports `AI_TUTOR_ENABLED = false`\n  with a comment naming the surfaces it gates and the date.\n- The constant gates **mounting and launching**, not just initial state:\n  - `renderTutorOverlay` returns `null` when the flag is off, regardless of\n    `tutorChatOpen`.\n  - `openTutorForCurrent`, the three wrong-answer launch sites\n    (`Drill.tsx:1849`, `:1957`, `:2105`) and the fixed-queue submit wrapper\n    (`:4261-4283`, which step 3 deletes anyway) all read\n    `AI_TUTOR_ENABLED && tutorMode`. `resolveTutorLaunch` gains a required\n    `flagEnabled` argument and returns `shouldOpen: false` when it is off.\n  - The legacy layout's tutor controls (`:4416-4431`) render only when the\n    flag is on.\n  - `tutorMode` initial state stays `true`; the flag is the master.\n    `tutorToggleVisible` on the frame stays `false` as today, so flipping\n    the flag on restores exactly the pre-change behaviour.\n- `DrillExplanationTab` receives `onAskTutor` only when the flag is on. Its\n  no-prose copy becomes: \"No written explanation was published for this\n  question.\"\n- `renderVoiceCoachChip` returns `null` when the flag is off.\n- `TutorChatModal` is not edited.\n\n### 2. Answer and Explanation become independent\n\nToday they are one switch with two reaches (`toggleExplanation` at\n`Drill.tsx:3625-3640` hides the Answer when Explanation closes, and opening\nExplanation goes through the reveal path; the drawer and Choice Notes also\ngate on `currentRevealed`).\n\n- New per-qid state `explanationOpenByQid: Map<string, boolean>`. In the\n  `reviewControlsAvailable` modes,\n  `explanationOpen = !!explanationOpenByQid.get(qid)` and the explanation\n  fetch is enabled by `explanationOpen`; elsewhere both stay\n  `notesOpen && drawerTab === 'explanation'` and `currentRevealed`, as now.\n  The strip receives `onShowAnswer` / `onExplanation` bound to the new\n  handlers only when `reviewControlsAvailable`, else the existing ones.\n- `toggleAnswerReveal(qid)`: flips `showAnswerRevealed[qid]` only. It never turns Explanation\n  off. `toggleExplanation(qid)` calls `openExplanation` when off; when on\n  it sets the entry `false` and closes the drawer if it is showing that\n  tab. The **pill** calls `toggleExplanation`; the **drawer tab**\n  (`onNotesPanelTabChange` at `:4088`, for `'explanation'` only) calls\n  `openExplanation`. `'details'` and `'notes'` still set the tab directly.\n  Same no-state-until-\"Show me\" rule for the Answer pill's confirm. Test\n  both Cancel paths.\n- **A pick on screen is recorded before a reveal.** In a fixed drill,\n  pressing Answer or Explanation with an uncommitted pick first calls\n  `commitPendingSelection` (the same function the right arrow uses) and\n  only then flips the pill. The four combinations\n  (off/off, Answer only, Explanation only, both) each have a test.\n- **Correctness is a fact, colouring is a state.** `InlineChoiceExplanation`\n  receives `isCorrect = correctAnswer === key` (factual), so with Answer off\n  the correct choice's note still says \"Why this is correct\". The pane's\n  existing `isCorrect` (which folds in `answerRevealed`) is renamed\n  `showCorrectColour` and used only for colour and the Check/X icon.\n\n### 3. Ovals keep their three states (current, answered,\n  jumpable); no correct/incorrect state is added (CONTEXT.md, Oval State).\n- The pick is recorded by navigation, as today: `officialNext`,\n  `officialPrev` and `handleJumpTo` all run `commitPendingSelection` before\n  moving. (`officialPrev` and `handleJumpTo` are audited to confirm they\n  do; if either skips it, it is added.)\n- End: the navigator has no End, so, exactly as a section does, the strip's\n  `onComplete` pill becomes the way out. The `usesSmartChrome ? undefined`\n  branch at `:4118` is changed so a fixed drill gets\n  `onComplete = handleFinishSection` with `completeLabel = 'End Drill'`.\n  `handleFinishSection` already commits a pending pick first (`:3868-3884`).\n- With Submit gone from fixed drills, nothing colours a choice\n  automatically; only the Answer pill does. The auto-reveal switch and\n  `autoReveal` preference are unchanged for Smart Drill and simply not\n  rendered in fixed drills (the navigator never had one).\n- `answerLocked` is no longer set to `true` by any reveal path in the\n  `reviewControlsAvailable` modes (`:1851`, `:1958`, `:1989`, `:2113`). When a verdict choice is *also* eliminated, the pane\n  renders a separate labelled `<button aria-label=\"Restore choice A\">`\n  (`EyeOff` icon) in the new 4th column, keyboard-focusable; pressing it\n  calls `onEliminate(key)`. Test: eliminate the correct choice, turn Answer\n  on, Restore is present and works.\n\n### 4. Picks after an attempt are practice\n\n- New state `practiceByQid: Map<string, { pick: string }>`. An entry\n  **exists** for a qid from the first post-attempt interaction (pick,\n  cross-out, or Reset) on; `pick: ''` is a cleared practice response and\n  is distinct from \"no entry\". So Reset on an attempted question stores\n  `{ pick: '' }` and can never fall back to the recorded answer.\n- One accessor, `effectiveSelection(qid)`:\n  `practiceByQid.get(qid)?.pick ?? selectedAnswer`. Never touches\n  `session.attempts` in these modes.\n- `commitPendingSelection` stays\n  `if (isAdaptiveMode || !selectedAnswer || currentAttempted || currentUnscored) return;`\n  so a practice pick is never persisted: no `persistAttempt`, no\n  `session.attempts` write, no journal, adaptive stats or resume rows.\n  Student work is never modified after the fact. ---
s a temporal-dead-zone `ReferenceError`.
   Fix: Declare the predicates after line 471 while keeping them above the handlers.

2. **Adaptive scoring can be gamed:** adaptive is included in `reviewControlsAvailable`, but only fixed drills commit a visible pick before Answer/Explanation; a student can reveal the authored answer, change the still-live choice, then Submit a falsely correct adaptive attempt.
   Fix: Atomically commit adaptive picks before either reveal, or require an existing attempt and treat no-attempt reveals as permanently unscored.

3. **The mode classifier violates the domain model:** ADR 0016 explicitly says `mode` is not the run kind—six distinct runs share `type-drill`—yet the predicate treats every non-section/non-adaptive run as a fixed drill.
   Fix: Classify with an exhaustive `DrillRunSpec.kind` switch and explicitly enumerate included and excluded run kinds.

4. **Bootcamp practice can transiently receive forbidden controls:** `isPracticeSetMode` starts as `false` and is established later, so an early render can classify a practice set as fixed despite the plan promising it remains untouched.
   Fix: Derive the policy synchronously from the resolved Drill Run Spec instead of mutable initialization state.

5. **End Drill loses the final pick:** the plan claims `handleFinishSection` commits pending selection, but [Drill.tsx:2545](C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\projects\lsatu\src\pages\Drill.tsx:2545) only consumes `attemptsOverride ?? session.attempts`; it never reads or saves `selectedAnswer`.
   Fix: Add an End wrapper that awaits `commitPendingSelection`, aborts on failure, and passes the returned attempts map to `handleFinishSection`.

6. **`effectiveSelection` is not actually the single source of truth:** the plan says every consumer uses it, then preserves a `commitPendingSelection` guard based on `selectedAnswer`; current next-button disabling also reads `selectedAnswer`.
   Fix: Compute `effectiveAnswer` for the current qid and use it in every guard, disabled prop, pane prop, reset handler, and commit path.

7. **The single-flight design deduplicates writes, not actions:** two rapid arrows can await the same promise and both navigate; arrow followed by End can both continue after the shared save and race navigation against completion.
   Fix: Lock the complete commit-plus-continuation action and test one navigation/completion, not merely one database insert.

8. **The pending-state representation is internally inconsistent:** a `Map<qid, Promise>` permits multiple concurrent qids, while `pendingCommitQid: string | null` represents only one and can re-enable controls while another commit remains pending.
   Fix: Use one global action flight or maintain a reactive `Set` of pending qids.

9. **Choices remain mutable during persistence:** while a delayed commit snapshots answer B, the student can select C or Reset; the eventual attempt records B while the visible state may imply C is the committed/practice response.
   Fix: Disable all response mutations and reveals for that qid during commit, or explicitly queue them as post-commit practice state.

10. **Previous/jump behavior remains ambiguous and easy to double-wrap:** `OfficialAnswerNavigator` invokes its callbacks directly, while current `officialPrev` and `handleJumpTo` do not commit; the plan simultaneously specifies raw callbacks and says they will commit.
    Fix: Introduce one `commitThenNavigate(destination)` wrapper and pass it exactly once for Previous, Next, and oval jumps.

11. **Explanation drawer state can leak across questions:** `explanationOpenByQid` is per-question, but `notesOpen` and `drawerTab` remain global; navigating from open A to closed B can leave the Explanation drawer visible while B’s pill and fetch are off.
    Fix: Synchronize the drawer on qid changes or derive its Explanation-open state from the current qid.

12. **The cross-out invariant is not implementable as written:** the plan says any post-attempt cross-out creates a practice entry, but its only writer runs when selection changes; crossing out an unrelated choice creates no entry.
    Fix: Call `ensurePracticeEntry(qid)` before every post-attempt pick, cross-out, or Reset mutation.

13. **The “borrow Standard” rule is unsupported by the schema:** neither `lr_explanations` nor its child tables records inheritance, so empty Brief/Another Way steps do not prove they are identical to Standard.
    Fix: Verify and document an ingestion invariant or add explicit inheritance metadata; otherwise render that style’s missing content honestly.

14. **A client constant is not a real AI Tutor kill switch:** hiding launch sites leaves the Edge Function callable directly by authenticated clients, so the proposed “master” switch does not control service use or spend.
    Fix: Enforce disablement server-side as well, with the client flag used only for presentation.

15. **The census predicate can drift from runtime behavior:** the plan duplicates whitespace-trimming logic between a JavaScript script and TypeScript hook without shared fixtures or conformance tests.
    Fix: Test both implementations against the same empty, whitespace, null, and mixed-style fixtures—or generate both from one shared pure module.

16. **Failure observability is toast-only:** blocked Next/Previous/Jump/End operations will be indistinguishable in production, with no action, run-kind, retry, or deduplication context.
    Fix: Emit a structured, non-PII commit-failure event containing qid, run kind, requested transition, error category, and retry/dedupe state.

17. **The tests omit the declared refresh semantics:** `practiceByQid` intentionally disappears on refresh, but no remount/resume test proves the recorded attempt returns without persisting the practice choice.
    Fix: Add a remount test asserting the logged answer and answered oval return, the practice pick disappears, and no attempt/journal/Run Record mutation occurred.

  const handlePracticeSetNext = () => {
    if (!session) return;
    
    // Pause timer on current question
    if (currentQuestion) {
      questionTimer.current.pause();
    }
    
    // Move to next
    if (session.currentIndex < session.questionQueue.length - 1) {
      setSession(prev => prev ? {
        ...prev,
        currentIndex: prev.currentIndex + 1,
      } : prev);
    } else {
      // Finished
      handleFinishPracticeSet();
    }
  };

  const handlePracticeSetPrevious = () => {
    if (!session) return;
    
    // Pause timer on current question
    if (currentQuestion) {
      questionTimer.current.pause();
    }
    
    // Move to previous. Functional, like every other queue move: stepping back
    // can follow an answer that was just committed, and a spread of the
    // closure's `session` would write that attempt straight back out.
    setSession(prev =>
      prev && prev.currentIndex > 0 ? { ...prev, currentIndex: prev.currentIndex - 1 } : prev,
    );
  };

  /**
   * Close out an Intro to LR practice set: record the module-progress

{"file_path": "C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu\\PLAN-post-question-explanation.md", "content": "# Plan: post-question Explanation, LSAT Lab shape, AI Tutor off\n_Locked via grill-with-docs on 2026-09-12 by Claude + Joshua. Revised after Codex rounds 1 to 5, re-cut after Joshua's clarification (fixed drills: arrows + bubbles, no Submit), revised again after Codex re-cut round 1. Terms per `src/components/drill/CONTEXT.md` (Answer, Explanation, Argument Breakdown, Choice Note, Reset Response, AI Tutor, Answer Navigator, Oval State, Drill Launch, Drill Run Spec)._\n\n## Goal\n\nOn the official drill surface (`/drill`), the student reviews a question the\nway LSAT Lab shows it. The **Answer** pill and the **Explanation** pill in\nthe session strip are independent on/off toggles. The **Argument Breakdown**\nsits under the stimulus (and is repeated in the drawer). Every **Choice\nNote** is a dropdown under its choice with a chevron. A **Reset Response**\ncontrol clears the pick. A fixed drill (guided drill, small drill, custom\nqueue) has no Submit pill: its bottom bar is arrows and ovals, as today,\nand nothing shows colour until the student presses Answer. The **AI\nTutor** is off for every student behind one switch, because the Question\nExplanation is authored LSAT Lab content already in Postgres\n(`lr_explanations`, `lr_explanation_steps`, `lr_explanation_choice_notes`)\nand the generated chat was failing on screen. ## user

Tool result:
The revision resolves most prior findings, but four blockers remain:

1. **`actionInFlight` is not a real lock.** A `useState<boolean>` update is asynchronous, so two clicks before rerender both see `false` and enter `commitThenNavigate`.
   Fix: Acquire a synchronous `useRef<boolean>` lock before the first `await`, with state used only to rerender disabled controls.

2. **Freeze-on-reveal is lost on refresh.** `revealedEverByQid` is explicitly memory-only, while Run Record snapshots omit `answerRevealed` (`Drill.tsx:868-887`); after remount, a previously revealed fixed-list answer becomes editable recorded work again.
   Fix: Persist an `answerRevealed`/frozen marker in the Run Record and reconstruct `revealedEverByQid` from it.

3. **Practice cross-outs still mutate recorded work.** `closeOpenVisit` copies `eliminatedRef` into `session.attempts` (`Drill.tsx:1254-1278`), so cross-outs made after freezing reach the attempt and resume snapshot despite the plan promising all frozen interactions are practice-only.
   Fix: Store frozen eliminations separately and make `closeOpenVisit` ignore practice-state eliminations.

4. **`questions` is not reliably a `fixed-list`.** `drillRunSpecFromLaunch` allows a `questions` launch to carry `natural-drill`; that initialization branch does not set `isPracticeSetMode`, so picks do not enter `session.attempts` immediately as the plan’s fixed-list policy assumes.
   Fix: Derive pick semantics independently from `isPracticeSetMode`, or classify `questions` from its resolved mode/config instead of unconditionally as `fixed-list`.

5. **`effectiveAnswer` breaks unfrozen fixed-commit revisits.** Its precedence is `practice → session attempt → selectedAnswer`; after returning to a committed-but-unrevealed question, a new selection written to `selectedAnswer` is masked by the existing attempt even though the plan says pre-freeze behavior remains unchanged.
   Fix: Either freeze fixed-commit questions upon commit or make the accessor/writer explicitly support visible pre-reveal revisions and define whether they can replace the persisted attempt.

6. **The End wrapper has no defined success result for fixed-list questions.** `commitPendingSelection` currently returns nothing when the question already has an attempt, while `commitThenNavigate` is specified to move only on success and only describes the returned attempts map for `fixed-commit`.
   Fix: Return a discriminated result such as `{ ok: true, attempts } | { ok: false }` for every no-op, fixed-list, unscored, and persisted path.

7. **The proposed failure event cannot supply `errorKind`.** `persistAttempt` catches the underlying exception and returns only `boolean` (`Drill.tsx:1512-1524`), discarding the information `reportCommitFailure` claims to report.
   Fix: Return a typed result carrying the failure category, or report the structured event inside the catch where the error still exists.

VERDICT: REVISE

## assistant

The plan is done. No open product decisions remain.

**What the plan does, in plain terms**

1. The AI Tutor is off for every student. One constant turns it back on.
2. The **Answer** pill and the **Explanation** pill each work on their own. Press to show, press again to hide. Neither locks the question.
3. When Explanation is on, the breakdown sits under the stimulus and in the drawer, and each choice has a dropdown with a chevron.
4. **Reset Response** clears the pick and touches nothing else.
5. Drills keep the arrows and the bubbles. The Submit pill and the auto-reveal switch go away in drills. Smart Drill is left alone.
6. One rule the review added: once the student has seen the answer for a question, her recorded answer is final. She can keep clicking; those clicks are practice and are never saved over her work. That rule survives a refresh.
7. A census file lists every question with no written explanation, by PrepTest, Section, Question.

**Files**
- [PLAN-post-question-explanation.md](PLAN-post-question-explanation.md), 480 lines
- [PLAN-REVIEW-LOG-post-question-explanation.md](PLAN-REVIEW-LOG-post-question-explanation.md): 7 Codex rounds, 53 findings, 51 accepted, 2 rejected with reasons
- [src/components/drill/CONTEXT.md](src/components/drill/CONTEXT.md): six new glossary terms

**One thing you did not decide, so I did (say if you disagree)**

A student who has *not* seen the answer can still change her pick in a drill, exactly as today. The freeze starts the first time she presses Answer or Explanation on that question.

**Next**

Say the word and I hand it to Codex to build (`/codex-build`, same log file, Claude reviews the diff and runs the proof). It builds on a fresh branch off `main` and opens a PR. Nothing is committed until then.

## user

Base directory for this skill: C:\Users\Joshu\.claude\skills\session-handoff

# Session Handoff

Produce a repeatable end-of-session summary so Joshua can `/clear` and start a fresh agent without losing continuity. The next agent should pick up by reading this summary alone.

This is a **context-handoff artifact**, not a status report. The audience is a future instance of you, not a stakeholder.

**Handoff vs. wrapup:** run `session-handoff` when the *context window* is the problem — nearing ~250k tokens (the dumb zone), or passing work between Claude and Codex. It is chat-only and writes nothing. Run `wrapup` when the *work day* is done — it strikes tasks, refreshes `wiki/hot.md`, and appends `wiki/log.md`. They can be run back to back (wrapup to persist, then handoff to reset).

## When to invoke

Joshua says: "session handoff", "hand off", "handoff summary", "summarize before I clear", or any near-equivalent. Note "let's wrap up" belongs to `wrapup`, not here — if he says that and the context window is also deep, run `wrapup` first, then offer this. Also invoke proactively if he says he's about to `/clear` without having run it, or if the session is clearly deep into the dumb zone.

## How to produce the summary

1. **Review the full conversation**, not just the last few turns. Handoffs miss things when they only summarize recent context.
2. **Pull state from these sources (in order):**
   - Plan files referenced this session (check `~/.claude/plans/` if a plan was mentioned).
   - TodoWrite state — any in-progress or pending tasks.
   - Background processes started with `run_in_background` — shell IDs are load-bearing for the next agent.
   - Files created or modified this session — you know what you touched; don't grep to re-discover.
   - Memory files written or updated (`~/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/`).
   - Unresolved questions — things you asked Joshua that never got a clear answer, or things he asked that got deflected.
3. **Do NOT audit the filesystem.** This is synthesis of what happened in THIS session. No `git log`, no broad `Glob` sweeps. If you didn't touch it this session, it doesn't belong here.
4. **Produce the output in chat.** Do not write a file. Do not update memory. Chat-only.

## Output template — use exactly this structure, every time

```
# Session Handoff — <one-line title of what this session was about>

## Where it started
<2-3 sentences: what Joshua asked for, key framing or constraints that emerged>

## Decisions locked + what shipped
- <decision or change> — <why, and where it lives (absolute path if a file)>
- ...

## Key files for next session
- `<absolute path>` — <why the next agent should read this first>
- Plan file: `<path>` (if a plan drove the session)
- Memory files touched: `<paths>` (if any)

## Running state
- Background processes: <shell IDs + what they are + how to kill> — or "none"
- Dev servers / ports: <url + port> — or "none"
- Open worktrees / branches: <paths> — or "none"

## Verified state as of <HH:MM>
- <claim about live/system state> — <how it was checked, and when>
- <claim> — NOT re-verified this session; last confirmed <when>
- ...

## Verification — how to confirm things still work
- `<command>` — <expected outcome>
- ...

## Deferred + open questions
- Deferred: <item> — <why pushed to later>
- OPEN (must be resolved or re-asked before new work): <question needing Joshua's input> — <context>

## Pick up here
<1-2 sentences: the single most likely next action for a fresh agent>
```

## Hard rules

1. **Chat output only.** Never write the handoff to a file. Never update memory from this skill. (That is `wrapup`'s job, not this one.)
2. **Never invent state.** If a section has nothing to report, write "none" — do not omit the section. Structure stability is the whole point.
3. **Absolute paths always.** The next agent may have a different working directory, or be Codex.
4. **If a plan file drove the session, name it first** in "Key files" so the next agent reads it before anything else.
5. **No emojis, no hype, no "great job" summaries.** Terse and concrete — paths, commands, shell IDs, decisions. Tone of a seasoned engineer handing off at end-of-shift.
6. **Background process IDs are critical.** If you started any `run_in_background` shells, their IDs must appear in "Running state" with the kill command — the next agent cannot find them otherwise.
7. **Stamp verified state, and separate it from assumed state.** Run `date "+%H:%M"` and use the real time. Every claim about live systems (deploy status, DB contents, what was sent, what is published) is either something you actually checked this session — say how — or explicitly marked NOT re-verified. A handoff that presents stale belief as current state makes the next session re-derive everything, which is the exact cost this skill exists to remove.
8. **Open questions are obligations, not notes.** Anything under OPEN must be resolved by the next session or re-asked to Joshua before it starts new work. Requirements have been silently re-typed across sessions, and a promised artifact once never arrived because nobody re-asked. If a question was open in the handoff you are picking up from, it is the first thing you raise.

## Anti-patterns — do not do these

- Summarizing the last 3 turns and calling it a handoff.
- Listing files by relative path.
- Skipping "Running state" because "nothing is running" — write "none" instead.
- Writing the summary to any file. This is chat-only by design.
- Adding a "what went well / what went poorly" retrospective. This isn't a retro.
- Recommending next steps beyond the single "Pick up here" line. The next agent decides; you just hand off.
